[000283] DIÁLOGO
There are traps placed around the dungeon.

[000284] DIÁLOGO
Most traps will activate when stepped on,

[000285] DIÁLOGO
causing balls and such to be shot at you.

[000286] DIÁLOGO
If you get hit by them, your status may change.

[000287] DIÁLOGO
Some cause damaging quakes or

[000288] DIÁLOGO
even spawn enemies.

[000289] DIÁLOGO
Watch out for traps under grass or ice.

[000290] DIÁLOGO
Traps disappear after activating a number of times.

[000291] DIÁLOGO
Brown treasure chests keep reappearing.

[000292] DIÁLOGO
Red ones appear only once.

[000293] DIÁLOGO
Black ones... are special.

[000294] DIÁLOGO
Each enemy has attributes based on colors.

[000295] DIÁLOGO
Yellow: Normal

[000296] DIÁLOGO
Green: High ATK

[000297] DIÁLOGO
White: High HP, DEF

[000298] DIÁLOGO
Red: ④ Element. Absorbs fire.

[000299] DIÁLOGO
Blue: ⑤ Element. Does not slip. Absorbs ice.

[000300] DIÁLOGO
Black: Really, really strong.

[000301] DIÁLOGO
Press the ⑯ Button at the dungeon to view

[000302] DIÁLOGO
your temporary EXP.

[000303] DIÁLOGO
When you reach the Results Screen, that EXP

[000304] DIÁLOGO
will be distributed among your party.

[000305] DIÁLOGO
A high level character won't get much EXP from

[000306] DIÁLOGO
defeating low level enemies. But, in the case of the

[000307] DIÁLOGO
opposite happening, they will get lots of EXP.

[000308] DIÁLOGO
If the main and sub do not have the same level,

[000309] DIÁLOGO
the EXP will be distributed accordingly.

[000326] DIÁLOGO

[000328] DIÁLOGO
They are the shields who protect the main.

Taking damage in battle

[000330] DIÁLOGO
will cause the subs to lose their HP.

[000331] DIÁLOGO

[000332] DIÁLOGO
Sub Character Space in the Magic Circle.

[000333] DIÁLOGO
The subs in the outer layer display first.

[000334] DIÁLOGO
The way the main faces corresponds to their positions.

[000335] DIÁLOGO

[000336] DIÁLOGO
will take damage first.

[000338] DIÁLOGO

[000339] DIÁLOGO
essentially become nullified.

[000340] DIÁLOGO
Artifacts can be placed

[000341] DIÁLOGO
in Artifact Spaces.

[000342] DIÁLOGO
Sub Characters will consume Mana

[000343] DIÁLOGO
to activate various Artifacts.

[000344] DIÁLOGO
Artifacts cannot be activated if there is no path

[000345] DIÁLOGO
connected to it.

[000346] DIÁLOGO

[000347] DIÁLOGO
exempt to this rule.

[000348] DIÁLOGO

[000349] DIÁLOGO
Ability Boost, SP Consumption Reduction,

[000350] DIÁLOGO
and Casting Time Reduction, are among those.

[000351] DIÁLOGO
Placing Artifacts in the same slot will allow

[000352] DIÁLOGO
effects to stack.

[000353] DIÁLOGO
Spaces with 'Widen' can double

[000354] DIÁLOGO
the effect of any Artifacts in it.

[000355] DIÁLOGO
Placing multiple Widen Artifacts in the same space

[000356] DIÁLOGO
will double, triple, or quadruple the effects.

[000357] DIÁLOGO
However, it will also consume Mana proportionately.

[000358] DIÁLOGO
Placing a Widen on a Magic/Ability Space will

[000359] DIÁLOGO
widen the range of the Magic/Ability.

[000360] DIÁLOGO
There are Fixed Spaces that have been set

[000361] DIÁLOGO
by default on the Magic Circle.

[000362] DIÁLOGO

[000363] DIÁLOGO

[000364] DIÁLOGO

[000365] DIÁLOGO
Double Death: Take out everyone else when dying.

[000366] DIÁLOGO
Mana xX: Provides a multiplier for your current Mana.

[000404] DIÁLOGO
Pressing the button at the right time

[000405] DIÁLOGO
allows you to perform a 3-hit combo.

[000406] DIÁLOGO
Has the property of ①.

[000407] DIÁLOGO
Hold the attack button for

[000408] DIÁLOGO
a charge attack.

[000409] DIÁLOGO
Has the property of ③.

[000410] DIÁLOGO
Can break destructible blocks.

[000411] DIÁLOGO
Allows you to use ranged attacks.

[000412] DIÁLOGO
Once the Staff Gauge on the bottom right

[000413] DIÁLOGO
is depleted, you must use melee attacks.

[000414] DIÁLOGO
Has the element of ④, ⑤ or ⑥.

[000415] DIÁLOGO
Elements are decided by the type of staff.

[000416] DIÁLOGO
The Staff Gauge gradually recovers automatically.

[000417] DIÁLOGO
While you are holding your shield, your DEF

[000418] DIÁLOGO
and element resistance can increase.

[000419] DIÁLOGO
It will be especially effective from the front.

[000420] DIÁLOGO
However, it becomes less effective as you use it.

[000421] DIÁLOGO
There are no stat differences between vendors.

[000422] DIÁLOGO
Armor can be shared with everyone.

[000423] DIÁLOGO
All equipment have titles.

[000424] DIÁLOGO
Normal is the default state.

[000425] DIÁLOGO
All titles have the ability

[000426] DIÁLOGO
to stack with one another.

[000427] DIÁLOGO
All equipment has ⑧ weight attributes.

[000428] DIÁLOGO
The higher the ⑧, the slower your attack.

[000434] DIÁLOGO
Shops have all kinds of equipment

[000435] DIÁLOGO
available for you to purchase.

[000436] DIÁLOGO
Powerful Artifacts are rarely dropped by

[000437] DIÁLOGO
enemies, so you should purchase those here.

[000438] DIÁLOGO
You can create new characters

[000439] DIÁLOGO
at the Tavern.

[000440] DIÁLOGO
You may also freely edit their faces,

[000441] DIÁLOGO
so be sure to make use of this.

[000442] DIÁLOGO
End the game through the treehole in the southeast.

[000443] DIÁLOGO
Be sure to save before you go.

What do you want to know about dungeons?

[000445] OPCIÓN
Traps

[000446] OPCIÓN
Treasure

[000447] OPCIÓN
Enemy Colors

[000448] OPCIÓN
EXP

[000449] OPCIÓN
Go back

[000450] DIÁLOGO
There are traps placed around the dungeon.

[000451] DIÁLOGO
Most traps will activate when stepped on,

[000452] DIÁLOGO
causing balls and such to be shot at you.

[000453] DIÁLOGO
If you get hit by them, your status may change.

[000454] DIÁLOGO
Some cause damaging quakes or

[000455] DIÁLOGO
even spawn enemies.

[000456] DIÁLOGO
Watch out for traps under grass or ice.

[000457] DIÁLOGO
Traps disappear after activating a number of times.

[000458] DIÁLOGO
Brown treasure chests keep reappearing.

[000459] DIÁLOGO
Red ones appear only once.

[000460] DIÁLOGO
Black ones... are special.

[000461] DIÁLOGO
Each enemy has attributes based on colors.

[000462] DIÁLOGO
Yellow: Normal

[000463] DIÁLOGO
Green: High ATK

[000464] DIÁLOGO
White: High HP, DEF

[000465] DIÁLOGO
Red: ④ Element. Absorbs fire.

[000466] DIÁLOGO
Blue: ⑤ Element. Does not slip. Absorbs ice.

[000467] DIÁLOGO
Black: Really, really strong.

[000468] DIÁLOGO
Press the ⑯ Button at the dungeon to view

[000469] DIÁLOGO
your temporary EXP.

[000470] DIÁLOGO
When you reach the Results Screen, that EXP

[000471] DIÁLOGO
will be distributed among your party.

[000472] DIÁLOGO
A high level character won't get much EXP from

[000473] DIÁLOGO
defeating low level enemies. But, in the case of the

[000474] DIÁLOGO
opposite happening, they will get lots of EXP.

[000475] DIÁLOGO
If the main and sub do not have the same level,

[000476] DIÁLOGO
the EXP will be distributed accordingly.

[000477] DIÁLOGO
What do you want to know about Magic Circles?

[000478] OPCIÓN
Magic Circles

[000479] OPCIÓN
Main Character

[000480] OPCIÓN
Sub Character

[000481] OPCIÓN
Artifact

[000482] OPCIÓN
Magic/Ability Space

[000483] OPCIÓN
Widening

[000484] OPCIÓN
Fixed Space

[000485] OPCIÓN
Go back

[000486] DIÁLOGO
This is a spell where the sub powers up the main

[000487] DIÁLOGO
by cheering him/her on.

[000488] DIÁLOGO
Subs consume Mana to activate

[000489] DIÁLOGO
Artifacts, which will then,

[000490] DIÁLOGO
in turn, power up the main.

[000491] DIÁLOGO
This is your playable character.

[000492] DIÁLOGO
When all subs get knocked out,

[000493] DIÁLOGO
the main's HP will display.

[000494] DIÁLOGO
When the main gets knocked out, it's Game Over.

[000495] DIÁLOGO
They are the shields who protect the main.

[000496] DIÁLOGO
Taking damage in battle

[000497] DIÁLOGO
will cause the subs to lose their HP.

[000498] DIÁLOGO
There is an outer layer and inner layer of the

[000499] DIÁLOGO
Sub Character Space in the Magic Circle.

[000500] DIÁLOGO
The subs in the outer layer are displayed first.

[000501] DIÁLOGO
The way the main faces corresponds to their positions.

[000502] DIÁLOGO
The subs on the outer side of the Magic Circle

[000503] DIÁLOGO
will start taking damage first.

[000504] DIÁLOGO
When the sub is knocked out, the Artifact

[000505] DIÁLOGO
he/she activated will

[000506] DIÁLOGO
essentially become nullified.

[000507] DIÁLOGO
Artifacts can be placed

[000508] DIÁLOGO
in Artifact Spaces.

[000509] DIÁLOGO
Sub Characters will consume Mana

[000510] DIÁLOGO
to activate various Artifacts.

[000511] DIÁLOGO
Artifacts cannot be activated if there is no path

[000512] DIÁLOGO
connected to it.

[000513] DIÁLOGO
Only the Artifact Anywhere Artifact is

[000514] DIÁLOGO
exempt to this rule.

[000515] DIÁLOGO
Place Artifacts that boost Magic/Abilities here.

[000516] DIÁLOGO
Ability Boost, SP Consumption Reduction,

[000517] DIÁLOGO
and Casting Time Reduction, are among those.

[000518] DIÁLOGO
Placing Artifacts in the same slot will allow

[000519] DIÁLOGO
its effects to stack.

[000520] DIÁLOGO
Spaces with 'Widen' can double

[000521] DIÁLOGO
the effect of any Artifacts in it.

[000522] DIÁLOGO
Placing multiple Widen Artifacts in the same space

[000523] DIÁLOGO
will double, triple, or quadruple the effects.

[000524] DIÁLOGO
However, it will also consume Mana proportionately.

[000525] DIÁLOGO
Placing a Widen on a Magic/Ability Space will

[000526] DIÁLOGO
Widen the range of the Magic/Ability.

[000527] DIÁLOGO
There are Fixed Spaces that have been set

[000528] DIÁLOGO
by default on the Magic Circle.

[000529] DIÁLOGO
Defend: Takes sub character's damage.

[000530] DIÁLOGO
HP -X: HP -X% of sub characters.

[000531] DIÁLOGO
Scatter X: Disperses X% of damage to subs.

[000532] DIÁLOGO
Double Death: Take out everyone else when dying.

[000533] DIÁLOGO
Mana xX: Provides a multiplier for your current Mana.

Controls

[000536] OPCIÓN
Magic

[000537] OPCIÓN

[000538] DIÁLOGO
Hold the ㈱ Button to run.

[000539] DIÁLOGO
Press the ㊧ Button while Running to Slide.

[000540] DIÁLOGO
Sliding allows you to slip under flying things.

[000541] DIÁLOGO
Press the ㊧ Button to hold up your shield.

[000542] DIÁLOGO
Press the ㊨ Button to Jump.

[000543] DIÁLOGO
Jumping allows you to get over traps.

[000544] DIÁLOGO
Press the ⑯ button to open the Menu.

[000545] DIÁLOGO
Select Give Up to quit the dungeon

[000546] DIÁLOGO
and return to Arcanus Cella.

[000547] DIÁLOGO
The money and EXP you obtained will be halved.

[000548] DIÁLOGO
Weapons you found will be confiscated.

[000549] DIÁLOGO
Press the ㈲ button during battle to select

[000550] DIÁLOGO
your magic or ability.

[000551] DIÁLOGO
You will fill your gauge, granting you the ability

[000552] DIÁLOGO
to use magic/ability if you have sufficient SP.

[000553] DIÁLOGO
This is a tutorial on the New-geon.

[000554] DIÁLOGO
What do you want to know?

[000555] OPCIÓN
New-geon

[000556] OPCIÓN
Super Move

[000557] OPCIÓN
Super Move explanation

[000558] OPCIÓN
G Star

[000559] OPCIÓN
Monster Level

[000560] OPCIÓN
Go back

[000561] DIÁLOGO
New-geons are Ran-geons with infinite depth.

[000562] DIÁLOGO
You will try to see how far you can get.

[000563] DIÁLOGO
Your record on where you reached will be saved.

[000564] DIÁLOGO
Each time you go through the gate in the New-geon,

[000565] DIÁLOGO
the Floor Jump numbers will fluctuate.

[000566] DIÁLOGO
The gate jumps past the number of floors shown.

[000567] DIÁLOGO
As a special rule, when the last two digits match,

[000568] DIÁLOGO
the Monster Level will double.

[000569] DIÁLOGO
The Floor Jump fluctuations adhere to rules.

[000570] DIÁLOGO
Be sure to avoid floors that have the same digits.

[000571] DIÁLOGO
The Super Move is also available to you in the New-geon.

[000572] DIÁLOGO
It will be set each time you pass through a gate.

[000573] DIÁLOGO
Press the ㊧+㊤ Buttons to activate.

[000574] DIÁLOGO
You will consume Floors to use the Super Move.

[000575] DIÁLOGO
Some Super Moves consume progressively more

[000576] DIÁLOGO
Floors while some are fixed.

[000577] DIÁLOGO
When you return to Arcanus Cella, that will reset.

[000578] DIÁLOGO
There are 5 types of Super Moves, one for each gate.

[000579] DIÁLOGO
Entering a gate of the same kind as the Super Move

[000580] DIÁLOGO
you have on will allow it to level up.

[000581] DIÁLOGO
Super Moves can level up to 9.

[000582] DIÁLOGO
The higher the level, the better its effect.

[000583] DIÁLOGO
When you return to Arcanus Cella, the Super Move

[000584] DIÁLOGO
you have on will reset back to Level 1.

[000585] DIÁLOGO
The Super Moves you do not have on will stay as is.

[000586] DIÁLOGO
Be sure to watch for how you level up your Super Moves.

[000587] DIÁLOGO
If you Give Up or your main gets knocked out,

[000588] DIÁLOGO
all Super Moves you have set will return to Level 0.

[000589] DIÁLOGO
The Super Moves you do not have on will stay the same.

[000590] DIÁLOGO
If you return to Arcanus Cella through the exit,

[000591] DIÁLOGO
the levels will stay the same.

[000592] DIÁLOGO
Normal Warrior (Floor Consumption 9 - Fixed)

[000593] DIÁLOGO
Warriors decrease consumption rate in your Circles.

[000594] DIÁLOGO
No trap activation. G will take longer to disappear.

[000595] DIÁLOGO
Gambler Merchant (Floor Consumption 1 - Fixed)

[000596] DIÁLOGO
Merchants increase any G that's scattered.

[000597] DIÁLOGO
Only applies to G being obtained.

[000598] DIÁLOGO
Angel Guard (Floor Consumption 10 - Doubles)

[000599] DIÁLOGO
Gets stronger relative to the number of Guardians.

[000600] DIÁLOGO
Heals all characters and revives downed subs.

[000601] DIÁLOGO
Demonic Sorcery (Floor Consumption 20 or 10 - Fixed)

[000602] DIÁLOGO
Powers up relative to the number of sorcerers.

[000603] DIÁLOGO
Replenishes SP and lowers Monster Level.

[000604] DIÁLOGO
Hell Dragoon (Floor Consumption 1,  +1 each use)

[000605] DIÁLOGO
Powers up relative to the number of dragoons.

[000606] DIÁLOGO
Deals damage to all enemies on-screen.

[000607] DIÁLOGO
Once activated, you will be in a special state

[000608] DIÁLOGO
called the G Star.

[000609] DIÁLOGO
The state will continue as long as you are on the floor.

[000610] DIÁLOGO
The amount of G you have boosts the main.

[000611] DIÁLOGO
Regardless of the main's base stats, the amount of G

[000612] DIÁLOGO
will increase damage dealt and decrease damage taken.

[000613] DIÁLOGO
There are some monsters in New-geon that

[000614] DIÁLOGO
are over LV 999.

[000615] DIÁLOGO
Defeat weaker enemies for them to scatter G

[000616] DIÁLOGO
before taking on the strong enemies.

[000617] DIÁLOGO
The Monster Level will be determined by the

[000618] DIÁLOGO
main character's starting ATK and DEF.

[000619] DIÁLOGO
(ATK+DEF)/4 will be the starting Monster Level.

What do you want to know?

[000622] OPCIÓN
Wireless Play

[000623] OPCIÓN
Start

[000624] OPCIÓN
Enter name

[000625] OPCIÓN
Create Co-op Room

[000626] OPCIÓN
Create Versus Room

[000627] OPCIÓN

[000628] OPCIÓN
Go back

[000629] DIÁLOGO
"Play CLADUN in Co-op or Versus mode"

[000630] DIÁLOGO
over a wireless connection

[000631] DIÁLOGO
with other PSP systems.

[000632] DIÁLOGO
Simply enter your name and you can begin.

[000633] DIÁLOGO
You will select a name used only for wireless play.

[000634] DIÁLOGO
You will be the host and create a room

[000635] DIÁLOGO
in which players will play in Co-op Mode.

[000636] DIÁLOGO
In Co-op Mode, you cannot hit other players.

[000637] DIÁLOGO
Clear the stage by having everyone reach the goal.

[000638] DIÁLOGO
You can revive downed players

[000639] DIÁLOGO
albeit with 1 HP.

[000640] DIÁLOGO
You will be the host and create a room

[000641] DIÁLOGO
in which players will play in Versus Mode.

[000642] DIÁLOGO
In Versus Mode, each player can hit each other.

[000643] DIÁLOGO
The surviving player to reach the goal wins.

[000644] DIÁLOGO
You can revive downed players

[000645] DIÁLOGO
albeit with 1 HP.

[000646] DIÁLOGO
Other players will play by

[000647] DIÁLOGO
joining the host's room.

[000657] DIÁLOGO
This 99 Floor dungeon changes shape all the time.

[000658] DIÁLOGO
Once you go in, you cannot escape unless you

[000659] DIÁLOGO
Give Up or find the Exit.

[000660] DIÁLOGO
Rare equipment, titles, and LV 99 monsters

[000661] DIÁLOGO
are said to be found here.

[000662] DIÁLOGO
Go through the usual Exit to quit the dungeon

[000663] DIÁLOGO
and return to Arcanus Cella.

[000664] DIÁLOGO
You cannot continue, though.

[000665] DIÁLOGO
This is the gate that takes you to the next floor.

[000666] DIÁLOGO
There are 5 types of gates.

[000667] DIÁLOGO
Regular Gate: Takes you to the next floor.

[000668] DIÁLOGO
Gamble Gate: Something good or bad will happen.

[000669] DIÁLOGO
Angel Gate: Something good will happen.

[000670] DIÁLOGO
Demon Gate: Something bad will happen.

[000671] DIÁLOGO
Hell Gate: The worst possible thing ever will happen.

[000672] DIÁLOGO
This is the average monster level for the next floor.

[000673] DIÁLOGO
It will increase as you pass through gates.

[000674] DIÁLOGO
Go through a bad gate and it might jump exponentially.

[000675] DIÁLOGO
This is the rate at which monsters drop items.

[000676] DIÁLOGO
The rate is low at first,

[000677] DIÁLOGO
but past 100, they will start to drop more.

[000678] DIÁLOGO
It can go up to 999.

[000679] DIÁLOGO
Increase it by using Angel Gates.

[000680] DIÁLOGO
This is the rate of good titles appearing.

[000681] DIÁLOGO
It can go up to 999.

[000682] DIÁLOGO
Increase it by using Angel Gates.

[001216] DIÁLOGO

[001217] DIÁLOGO
"Status effects"

[001218] DIÁLOGO
Some attacks or traps can deal status effects.

[001219] DIÁLOGO
All status effects heal over time.

[001220] DIÁLOGO
Poison: Lose 5% of HP every few seconds.

[001221] DIÁLOGO
Sleep: Unable to move until you take damage.

[001222] DIÁLOGO
Blind: The entire map becomes dark.

[001223] DIÁLOGO
Burn: Attacks get ④ element, but you take damage.

[001224] DIÁLOGO
Naked: Nothing equipped but a smile.

[001225] DIÁLOGO
Haste: Movement speed is increased.

[001226] DIÁLOGO
Slow: Movement speed is decreased.

[001227] DIÁLOGO
ATK +/-: Shows change in ATK. Can be stacked.

[001228] DIÁLOGO
DEF +/-: Shows change in DEF. Can be stacked.

[001229] DIÁLOGO
Regenerate: Heals HP every few seconds.

[001230] DIÁLOGO
"LV Up"

[001231] DIÁLOGO
Defeat enemies to get EXP for all active members.

[001232] DIÁLOGO

[001233] DIÁLOGO

[001234] DIÁLOGO
a sub character or a main character.。

[001235] DIÁLOGO
"LV Up as the main character."

[001236] DIÁLOGO
You will gain stats that make you a better sub.

[001237] DIÁLOGO
The character gains more HP and Mana.

[001238] DIÁLOGO
"LV Up as a sub character."

[001239] DIÁLOGO
You will gain stats that make you a better main.

[001240] DIÁLOGO
The character gains more ATK and DEF.

[001241] DIÁLOGO
In other words, it's best to keep switching roles

[001242] DIÁLOGO
around so you can become more effective

[001243] DIÁLOGO
at each character's particular role.

[001244] DIÁLOGO
Characters you want to use as mains should be

[001245] DIÁLOGO
leveled up as subs and vice-versa.

[001246] DIÁLOGO

[001247] DIÁLOGO

[005451] INTERFAZ
　EXP Growth△　　○○Growth△　 Fixed Space　Slot

[005452] INTERFAZ
The LV shows how strong the character is.
As you accumulate EXP, your stats will go up.

[005453] INTERFAZ
HP are your hit points.When a sub's HP falls to 0,
they will disappear.When the main's HP hits 0, 
you will be returned to Arcanus Cella.

[005454] INTERFAZ
SP is needed to use magic or abilities.
It will not recover during battle.

[005455] INTERFAZ
ATK is your attack power.
The higher your ATK, the more damage you deal.
Orbs from staves are not dependent on ATK.

[005456] INTERFAZ
DEF is your defense.
The higher your DEF, the less damage you take.

[005457] INTERFAZ
Magic/Ability can be set on your Magic Circle for 
battle use.Boost them on the "Magic/Ability Space" 
of the Magic Circle.

[005458] INTERFAZ
CRT is the Critical Rate.
The higher the CRT, the more often critical hits occur.
Critical hits deal more damage than usual.

[005459] INTERFAZ
BRK is the break rate.The higher the BRK, the more 
likely you are to break enemies.Breaking enemies 
will render them motionless for a while.

[005460] INTERFAZ
Explanation of ⑦ ※Unused (Do not delete)

[005461] INTERFAZ
The combined weight of you and your equipment.
The higher the number, the slower your attacks.

[005462] INTERFAZ
Defensive power of your shield.
This reduces damage when you have a shield up.

[005463] INTERFAZ
① is your resistance to the "Slash①" property.
100 is normal. The higher it is, the more resistant
you are.The less it is, the more vulnerable you are.

[005464] INTERFAZ
③ is your resistance to the "Blunt③" property.
100 is normal. The higher it is, the more resistant
you are.The less it is, the more vulnerable you are.

[005465] INTERFAZ
② is your resistance to the "Pierce②" property.
100 is normal. The higher it is, the more resistant
you are.The less it is, the more vulnerable you are.

[005466] INTERFAZ
④ is your resistance to the "Fire④" element.
100 is normal. The higher it is, the more resistant
you are.The less it is, the more vulnerable you are.

[005467] INTERFAZ
⑤ is your resistance to the "Ice⑤" element.
100 is normal. The higher it is, the more resistant
you are.The less it is, the more vulnerable you are.

[005468] INTERFAZ
⑥ is your resistance to the "Spirit⑥" element.
100 is normal. The higher it is, the more resistant
you are.The less it is, the more vulnerable you are.

[005469] INTERFAZ
⑳① is your resistance to the
"Slash①" property when holding a shield.

[005470] INTERFAZ
⑳③ is your resistance to the
"Blunt③"property when holding a shield.

[005471] INTERFAZ
⑳② is your resistance to the
"Pierce②" property when holding a shield.

[005472] INTERFAZ
⑳④ is your resistance to the
"Fire④" element when holding a shield.

[005473] INTERFAZ
⑳⑤ is your resistance to the
"Ice⑤" element when holding a shield.

[005474] INTERFAZ
⑳⑥ is your resistance to the
"Spirit⑥" element when holding a shield.

[005475] INTERFAZ
Your walking speed.
The higher this is, the faster you walk.

[005476] INTERFAZ
Your running speed.
The higher this is, the faster you run.

[005477] INTERFAZ
All equipment have titles.
Different titles provide different effects and powers.

[005478] INTERFAZ
Power(%) is your weapon strength.
If this is low, you cannot deal damage, 
no matter how high your ATK.

[005479] INTERFAZ
Your job.The type of Magic Circle, magic, 
and abilities differ based on your job.
What stats upgrade upon a level up also change.

[005480] INTERFAZ
Points used to activate Artifacts.
Utilize your subs' mana to boost the main.
Plan wisely.
